- **forge**: Description: Adversarial code hardening for Claude Code. Forge spawns two agents — a Builder and a Breaker — that iterate against each other until the code is unbreakable. The Builder implements a feature, the Breaker writes adversarial tests to prove bugs, and the Builder must fix the implementation without deleting or weakening the tests.
